1. Evaluate parent, keep best.

4. Validate best surrogate top candidates with exact evaluate_sequence if time, return best exact.

Parametric candidate:
```
def make_fourier(params, n):
  theta=2π*i/n
  a=1
  for m,A,phi: a += A*np.cos(m*theta+phi)
  a=np.clip(a,0,None)
  s=a.sum(); if s<1: a=1
  a*= n/s
  return a
```
Bounds A∈[0,1.2] maybe; clip ensures nonneg. But if amplitudes large, support shrinks and score maybe high; optimizer can adapt.

Let's design a reliable optimization routine:
```
def optimize_params(params0, method='pgrad'):
    best_p = params0; best_s = surrogate_score(make_fourier(best_p))
    n_params = len(best_p)
    # Coordinate descent with adaptive step
    while time < deadline:
       improved=False
       for j in range(n_params):
          # current value, bounds maybe [-1.2,1.2]
          base = best_p[j]
          for step in [0.01, -0.01, 0.05, -0.05, 0.1, -0.1, 0.2, -0.2, 0.5, -0.5]:
             p=best_p.copy(); p[j]=base+step; val=score(make_fourier(p)); if val<best_s: accept
          # if no single step, try random offsets limited
       if no improvement and time remains: random jump one param
    return
```
But coordinate descent with step list may be slow if each eval. We can use `scipy.optimize.minimize` with 'Nelder-Mead' which uses ~dim*eval per iter, efficient C? It's Python but okay. It may get stuck; use multiple starts.

while _L < 2*n-1: _L <<= 1
_omega = 1j*2*np.pi/_L? not needed.
def fft_conv(a):

    b = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, _L)[:2*n-1]
    # clip small negative
    np.maximum(b,0,out=b)

```

- surrogate_score:
```
def score_from_b(a,b):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    return float(2*n*b.max()/(s*s))
```
We can normalize a to sum n before score to use `2*b.max()/n`; but during candidate generation clip maybe sum not n. Just compute actual.

- To speed many param candidates, normalize to sum n: `a *= n / s` (if s>0). Then score = 2*bmax/n. This avoids divide S^2 and overflow. Need ensure a values finite within 1000; if normalization makes max >1000? Generate f O(1) so no. For raw, maybe.

- Candidate generation using precomputed theta and cos/sin tables for harmonics to speed:
```
m_max=4
freqs=[]
for m in range(1,m_max+1):
  ang = 2*np.pi*m*np.arange(n)/n
  freqs.append((np.cos(ang), np.sin(ang)))
def make_from_z(z, offset=0):
  amp = 1.0
  if offset: shift index? ignore for speed
  for idx,(c,s) in enumerate(freqs):
     re=z[2*idx]; im=z[2*idx+1]
     # 2*Re(z_m e^{-imθ}) = 2(re c + im s)
     amp += 2*(re*c + im*s)
  np.clip(amp,0,None,out=amp)
  s=amp.sum()
  if s<=0: amp=np.ones(n)
  else: amp *= n/s
  return amp
```
This uses precomputed c,s arrays length n for m=1..M (4*4806*2 floats ~300KB). Fine.

Offset parameter: we can generate with `np.roll(amp, shift)`? Convolution shifted? Shifting entire sequence does not change linear convolution peak? If wrap? A linear shift within length changes convolution because endpoints zero padded, not cyclic. So offset matters. We can incorporate by generating theta with i+offset: too costly per candidate. Instead allow one `roll` after generation? `np.roll` on full vector (cyclic shift) not equivalent to linear shift (it moves zeros? Actually rolling changes boundary discontinuity; might improve/worsen). We can try a few offsets by slicing/rolling the nonnegative vector: `np.roll(amp, off, axis=0)` preserves sum and nonnegative, linear convolution changes (cyclic roll effectively changes the placement relative to boundaries). Since sequence length fixed, any cyclic shift is valid. For a smooth periodic-like function, cyclic shift changes boundary effect; we can test offsets [0,120,1600,...]. Easy: in candidate eval, for a few offsets: `for off in offset_list: cand=np.roll(amp, off)`. This may find better edge effects. Include offsets [0, n//5, n//4, n//3, random].

Could use active gradient with top K and line search. Let's code carefully.

```
def raw_refine(a, deadline, max_iter=None):
    n=a.size
    a=a.copy().astype(float)
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return a
    a *= n/s
    best=a.copy(); best_score=surrogate_score(a) # normalized
    # Precompute active weights? no
    rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b, M = conv(a)
        if M < best_score/n? actually surrogate_score returns 2*M/n.
        # choose active indices: top K = 64 or threshold
        K=min(128, len(b))
        # use np.argpartition for top K faster
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]  # not sorted
        # Weight: w = b[idx] - b.max() + 1? Use softmax maybe:
        wm = (b[idx] / M) ** 8  # emphasize highest; values close 1
        h = np.zeros(n)
        for k,w in zip(idx, wm):
           start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(k,n-1)
           if end>=start:
              h[start:end+1] += w * a[k-start : k-end-1 : -1]
        # If h max ==0, random perturbation
        if h.max() > 0:
           d = - (h - h.mean())
           dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
           if dmax>0:
              d /= dmax
              improved=False
              for alpha in (1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,1e-1,3e-1):
                  cand=np.clip(a + alpha*d, 0, None)
                  sc=cand.sum()
                  if sc<=0: continue
                  cand *= n/sc
                  val=surrogate_score(cand)
                  if val < best_score - 1e-9:
                      best_score=val; best=cand.copy(); a=cand.copy(); improved=True; break
              if improved: continue
        # random smooth perturbation
        for _ in range(10):
            # random Fourier in low dims added to normalized a
            noise = np.zeros(n)
            for r in range(1,4):
               amp = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02*r?)? Need alpha.
            # better: noise = rng.normal(0, sigma, n) then low-pass? We have freq tables: use random z small:
            z = rng.normal(0, 0.05, 2*M) # current? But make_from_z rebuilds from scratch, not additive.
```

```
def arr_from_z(z):
    a=np.ones(n)
    for m in range(Mmax):
       re=z[2*m]; im=z[2*m+1]
       if re or im:
          a += 2.0*(re*coss[m] + im*sins[m])
    np.clip(a,0.0,None,out=a)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.ones(n)
    if s!=n: a *= n/s
    return a
```
For optimization, objective uses this. Note if z large clip changes mean, normalization; non-smooth.

Self-contained parameter search:
- Maintain list of top params? Simulated annealing over continuous z.
- For each start, do `num_evals` random direction steps:
  `p = p + step * gaussian(reason)`; clip; evaluate; if better, accept; else Metropolis accept with probability exp(-(score-best)/T). But objective deterministic. We can just random hill climbing: each step, perturb one parameter by small amount; if better accept. Use multiple step sizes. This is easy.

Pseudo:
```
def fmin_coords(p0, max_time):
  p=p0.copy(); s=obj(p)
  step_sizes={j:0.1 for j}
  for eval in range(maxfev):
    j=random param
    delta=choice(±1)*step_sizes[j]*choice([0.2,0.5,1,2])
    p[j]=clip(p[j]+delta)
    s2=obj(p)
    if s2 < s * (1-1e-7): s=s2; step_sizes[j]*=1.2
    else: p[j]=old; step_sizes[j]*=0.8
  return p,s
```

Concretely:
```
def refine_raw(a, deadline, verbose=False):
    n = a.size
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return a
    a *= n/s
    best=a.copy(); bs=score_array(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(999)
    b=None; M=np.inf
    no_improve=0
    temperature=0.02
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = conv(a); M=b.max()
        cur=score_array_from_M(M) # 2*M/n
        if cur < bs: ... # though cur from a should equal
        # active top K
        K=64
        idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        w=(b[idx]/M)**10 if M>0 else ones
        h=np.zeros(n)
        for k,wi in zip(idx,w):
            st=max(0,k-n+1); en=min(k,n-1)
            if en>=st:
               h[st:en+1] += wi * a[k-st : k-en-1 : -1]
        if h.max()>0:
            d = - (h - h.mean())
            dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
            if dmax>0:
                d/=dmax
                improved=False
                # adaptive alphas
                alphas=(1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,1e-1)
                for al in alphas:
                    cand=np.clip(a + al*d, 0, None)
                    sc=cand.sum()
                    if sc <= 0: continue
                    cand *= n/sc
                    val=score_array(cand)
                    if val < best_s - 1e-10:
                        best_s=val; best=cand.copy(); a=cand.copy(); improved=True; break
                    elif val < cur + temperature*best_s:
                        # accept as current to explore? only if not too worse
                        a=cand.copy()
                        break
                if improved: no_improve=0; continue
        # random perturbations
        improved=False
        for scale in (1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2):
            noise=np.zeros(n)
            # random low freq up to 3
            for m in range(3):
                amp = scale * (m+1) * rng.standard_normal(2)
                noise += 2*(amp[0]*coss[m] + amp[1]*sins[m])
            cand=np.clip(a + noise,0,None)
            sc=cand.sum(); if sc<=0: continue
            cand *= n/sc
            val=score_array(cand)
            if val < best_s:
                best_s=val; best=cand.copy(); a=cand.copy(); improved=True; break
            elif val < best_s * (1+0.02):
                a=cand.copy(); break
        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            temperature = min(0.2, temperature*1.2)
            if no_improve > 200: break
    return best,best_s
```

def score_array(a, b=None):
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 0: return inf
   if b is None: b=conv(a)
   return float(2*n*b.max()/(s*s))
```

Let's design exact code structure:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try:
        n=int(height_sequence_1.size)
        parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        n=4806
        parent=np.ones(n)
    parent=np.clip(parent,0,1000)
    if parent.size==0: parent=np.ones(n)
    n=parent.size
    # maybe reduce if n too large? no
    if n%2 ...? Use as is.
    parent *= n/parent.sum() if sum>0 else ones
    # FFT setup
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    def conv(a):
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
        np.maximum(b,0,out=b)
        return b
    def score(a,b=None):
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=0: return inf
       if b is None: b=conv(a)
       m=b.max()
       if m<=0: return 0.0
       if abs(s-n) <= 1e-6*max(1,s): return float(2*m/n) # because s=n
       return float(2*n*m/(s*s))
    # freq tables M=4
    Mh=4
    idx=np.arange(n)
    coss=[]; sins=[]
    for m in range(1,Mh+1):
       ang=2*np.pi*m*idx/n
       coss.append(np.cos(ang)); sins.append(np.sin(ang))
    NB=2*Mh
    def arr_from_z(z):
       a=np.ones(n)
       z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
       for m in range(Mh):
          re=z[2*m]; im=z[2*m+1]
          if re!=0 or im!=0:
             a += 2*(re*coss[m] + im*sins[m])
       np.clip(a,0.0,None,out=a)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=0: return np.ones(n)
       a *= n/s
       if a.max()>1000: a *= 1000/a.max()
       return a
    best = parent.copy(); best_s=score(best)
    def consider(a):
       nonlocal best,best_s
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
       if a.size!=n or not np.isfinite(a).all() or a.min()< -1e-9: return
       np.clip(a,0,None,out=a)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=1e-9: return
       a *= n/s
       if a.max()>1000: a*=1000/a.max()
       sc=score(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_s:
          best_s=sc; best=a.copy()
    consider(parent)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline_param = t0 + min(120.0, max(20.0, float(budget_s)*0.08)) ?
```

Let's outline final code in detail:

```
import numpy as np, time
try: from scipy.optimize import minimize
except: minimize=None

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    B=float(max(1.0,budget_s))
    hard=t0+B-5.0
    try:
       parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       parent=np.ones(4806)
    if parent.size==0:
       parent=np.ones(4806)
    n=parent.size
    parent=np.clip(parent,0,1000.0)
    ps=parent.sum()
    if ps>0: parent = parent * (n/ps)
    else: parent=np.ones(n)
    L=1
    while L<2*n-1: L<<=1
    conv_len=2*n-1
    def conv(a):
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
       b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:conv_len]
       if b.size: np.maximum(b,0,out=b)
       return b
    def score(a, b=None):
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return float('inf')
       if b is None: b=conv(a)
       m=float(b.max())
       if m<=0: return 0.0
       if not np.isfinite(m): return inf
       if abs(s-n) <= 1e-6*max(1.0,s):
          return float(2.0*m/n)
       return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
    def normalize(a):
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
       if a.size!=n: return None
       np.clip(a,0.0,None,out=a)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=1e-12: return None
       a = a * (n/s)
       mx=a.max()
       if mx>1000.0:
           a = a * (1000.0/mx)
       if not np.isfinite(a).all(): return None
       return a
```
`normalize` returns new array maybe; if we mutate? fine.

Refs tables Mh=3:
```
Mh=3; NB=2*Mh
idx=np.arange(n)
coss=[np.cos(2*np.pi*m*idx/n) for m in range(1,Mh+1)]
sins=[np.sin(2*np.pi*m*idx/n) for m in range(1,Mh+1)]
def arr_from_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float).copy()
   z=np.clip(z,-0.8,0.8)
   a=np.ones(n)
   for m in range(Mh):
      re=z[2*m]; im=z[2*m+1]
      if re or im: a += 2.0*(re*coss[m]+im*sins[m])
   return normalize(a)
```
If z length < NB, pad zeros. Use if z.size<NB.

def consider(a, z=None):
   nonlocal best,best_score
   a=normalize(a)
   if a is None: return
   sc=score(a)
   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
       best_score=sc; best=a.copy()
   if z is not None: record_top(z,sc)
```

def raw_refine(a0, deadline):
   a=normalize(a0)
   if a is None: return a0, best_score
   cur_score=score(a)
   local_best=a.copy(); local_best_score=cur_score
   if cur_score < best_score: best_score=cur_score; best=a.copy()
   rng2=np.random.default_rng(seed+1)
   no_best=0
   temp=0.02
   Mh_local=3
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       b=conv(a)
       bmax=float(b.max())
       if not finite: break
       cur_score = 2*bmax/a.sum()**2*n? use score(a,b)

```

           local_best_score=cur_score; local_best=a.copy()

           if en>=st:

       improved=False

    if a is None: return None
    if a.size==0: return None
    if not np.isfinite(a).all(): return None

Let's design a dedicated small-n optimizer.

Exact dense score:
```
def score_dense(a):
   s=a.sum()
   if s <=0: return inf
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   return float(2*len(a)*b.max()/(s*s))
```

Function `optimize_length(n, seed, time_budget)`:
- `b_len=2n-1`.
- Use dense score with sum normalized to 1: `score1(a) = 2*n*b.max()` (since S=1). But if we use `score_dense` general, scale invariant.
- Normalize to sum=1 for optimization to keep values small. Final scale arbitrary; returning sum1 values fine (sum may be 1, evaluator sum>=0.01). Coefficients ~1/n <1000.
- Local refine function:
```
def refine(a, max_iter, deadline):
   a=a.copy(); a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return a
   a/=s
   best=a.copy(); best_m=b.max(); best_score=2*n*best_m
   for it in range(max_iter):
      b=np.convolve(a,a); M=b.max()
      if 2*n*M < best_score: update
      # active top K = min(8, 2n-1)
      idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
      h=np.zeros(n)
      for k in idx:
         st=max(0,k-n+1); en=min(k,n-1)
         stop=k-en; stop_slice=None if stop<=0 else stop-1
         h[st:en+1] += a[k-st:stop_slice:-1]
      d=-(h-h.mean()); dmax=np.max(abs(d)); if dmax==0 break
      d/=dmax
      best_alpha=None
      for al in [1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,1e-1]:
         cand=a+al*d; cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cs=cand.sum(); if cs<=0: continue; cand/=cs
         b2=np.convolve(cand,cand); sc=2*n*b2.max()
         if sc < best_score:
             a=cand; best_score=sc; best=cand.copy(); break
      # if no improvement, random small perturbation:
      else: add noise and if sc < best_score or < current+temp accept.
   return best,best_score
```
Since n small, line search 7*conv; 7* small. Number iterations maybe 1000 per start; 50 starts=50k conv; n=64 conv 8k operations =>400M C, ok.

```
def make_start(n, st, rng):
    i=np.arange(n)
    if st == 0:
       a=np.ones(n)
    else:
       kind = st % 9
       if kind==1: a=np.linspace(1.0,n,n)
       elif kind==2: a=np.linspace(n,1.0,n)
       elif kind==3: a=np.exp(np.linspace(0.0,1.5,n))
       elif kind==4: a=np.exp(np.linspace(1.5,0.0,n))
       elif kind==5: a=1.0+0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*i/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       elif kind==6: a=1.0+0.5*np.cos(4*np.pi*i/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       elif kind==7: a=1.0+0.7*np.cos(2*np.pi*i/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.4*np.cos(4*np.pi*i/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       elif kind==8: a=rng.random(n)+0.01
       else: a=np.abs(rng.normal(0,1,n))+0.01
       # diversity noise
       a = a + 0.05*rng.normal(0,1,n)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)
    else: a=a/s
    return a
```
For n=1, `np.linspace(1,1,1)` okay. `i/n` 0. cos.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    B = float(max(1.0, float(budget_s)))
    hard = t0 + max(0.5, B - 15.0)
    opt_end = t0 + max(1.0, B - 180.0) if B > 300 else t0 + max(0.5, B * 0.7)
    opt_end = min(opt_end, hard - 30.0)
    polish_deadline = hard - 10.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    if parent.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(parent).all():
        parent = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ps = parent.sum()
    if ps > 0.0:
        scale = parent.size / ps
        if parent.max() * scale <= 1000.0:
            parent = parent * scale
    parent_list = parent.astype(float).tolist()

    best_list = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    top = []

    def score_array(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
        n = a.size
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return float("inf")
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        m = float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            return float("inf")
        return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))

    def consider(a):
        nonlocal best_list, best_score
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
        if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all() or a.max() <= 0:
            return
        sc = score_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_list = a.astype(float).copy()

    def add_top(sc, a):
        if a is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        top.append((sc, a.astype(float).copy(), a.size))
        if len(top) > 40:
            top.pop(max(range(len(top)), key=lambda i: top[i][0]))

    consider(np.ones(2, dtype=float))

    def softmax(v):
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
        v = np.clip(v, -12.0, 12.0)
        x = v - v.max()
        e = np.exp(x)
        s = e.sum()
        if s <= 0.0:
            return np.full(v.size, 1.0 / v.size)
        return e / s

    def make_start(n, st):
        i = np.arange(n)
        if st == 0:
            a = np.ones(n)
        else:
            kind = st % 9
            if kind == 1:
                a = np.linspace(1.0, n, n)
            elif kind == 2:
                a = np.linspace(n, 1.0, n)
            elif kind == 3:
                a = np.exp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.5, n))
            elif kind == 4:
                a = np.exp(np.linspace(1.5, 0.0, n))
            elif kind == 5:
                a = 1.0 + 0.80 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * i / n + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            elif kind == 6:
                a = 1.0 + 0.50 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * i / n + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            elif kind == 7:
                a = (1.0 + 0.70 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * i / n + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)) +
                     0.40 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * i / n + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
            elif kind == 8:
                a = rng.random(n) + 0.01
            else:
                a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)) + 0.01
            a = a + 0.05 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
        s = a.sum()
        if s <= 0.0:
            a = np.ones(n)
        else:
            a = a / s
        return a

    def top_idx(b, K):
        K = max(1, min(K, b.size))
        if K >= b.size:
            return np.arange(b.size), b
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        return idx, b[idx]

    def objective(v, n, beta, K):
        a = softmax(v)
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        bmax = float(b.max())
        idx, bt = top_idx(b, K)
        z = beta * (bt - bmax)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        es = ez.sum()
        val = bmax + np.log(es) / beta
        p = ez / es
        g = np.zeros(n)
        for k, w in zip(idx, p):
            if w < 1e-14:
                continue
            st = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
            en = min(int(k), n - 1)
            if en >= st:
                stop = int(k) - en
                stop_slice = None if stop <= 0 else stop - 1
                g[st:en + 1] += w * a[int(k) - st:stop_slice:-1]
        gv = g - a * float(np.dot(g, a))
        scale = 2.0 * n
        return float(scale * val), scale * gv

    def polish(a, n, deadline, max_iter=400):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = a.sum()
        if s <= 0:
            return a, float("inf")
        a = a / s
        best = a.copy()
        best_sc = score_array(a)

        def sc1(x):
            x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
            x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
            ss = x.sum()
            if ss <= 0.0:
                return float("inf"), None
            x = x / ss
            b = np.convolve(x, x)
            return float(2.0 * n * b.max()), x

        fail = 0
        for _ in range(max_iter):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            b = np.convolve(a, a)
            m = float(b.max())
            cur = 2.0 * n * m
            if cur < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur
                best = a.copy()
                fail = 0
            else:
                fail += 1

            idx, bt = top_idx(b, 8)
            w = (bt / max(m, 1e-300)) ** 4
            h = np.zeros(n)
            for k, wi in zip(idx, w):
                if wi < 1e-12:
                    continue
                st = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
                en = min(int(k), n - 1)
                if en >= st:
                    stop = int(k) - en
                    stop_slice = None if stop <= 0 else stop - 1
                    h[st:en + 1] += wi * a[int(k) - st:stop_slice:-1]

            moved = False
            if h.max() > 0.0:
                d = -(h - h.mean())
                dm = np.max(np.abs(d))
                if dm > 0.0:
                    d /= dm
                    for al in (1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2):
                        cand, sc2 = sc1(a + al * d)
                        if cand is None:
                            continue
                        if sc2 < best_sc:
                            best_sc = sc2
                            best = cand.copy()
                            a = cand
                            moved = True
                            break
                        if sc2 < cur + 0.01 * best_sc:
                            a = cand
                            moved = True
                            break

            if not moved:
                noise = (1e-3 * (1.0 + 0.05 * fail)) * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
                cand, sc2 = sc1(a + noise)
                if cand is not None:
                    if sc2 < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc2
                        best = cand.copy()
                        a = cand
                        moved = True
                    elif sc2 < best_sc * 1.02:
                        a = cand
            if fail > 120 and not moved:
                break
        return best, best_sc

    specs = [
        (2, 8), (3, 8), (4, 10), (5, 10), (6, 12), (7, 12),
        (8, 18), (10, 18), (12, 20), (14, 18), (16, 24),
        (20, 20), (24, 18), (32, 30), (48, 18), (64, 14), (96, 8), (128, 6)
    ]
    total_w = float(sum(w for _, w in specs))
    spec_time = max(0.0, opt_end - t0)
    cur = t0

    for n, w in specs:
        if n <= 1 or time.monotonic() >= opt_end:
            continue
        n_time = spec_time * (w / total_w)
        n_deadline = min(opt_end, cur + max(2.0, n_time))
        cur = n_deadline

        max_starts = 150 if n <= 16 else 100 if n <= 32 else 60 if n <= 64 else 40
        maxiter = 300 if n <= 16 else 200 if n <= 48 else 120
        beta = max(10.0, 5.0 * float(n))
        K = min(96, 2 * n - 1)
        bounds = [(-12.0, 12.0)] * n

        for st in range(max_starts):
            if time.monotonic() >= n_deadline:
                break
            a0 = make_start(n, st)
            consider(a0)
            v0 = np.log(a0 + 1e-12)
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    lambda v: objective(v, n, beta, K),
                    v0,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": maxiter * 4, "ftol": 1e-9, "gtol": 1e-6},
                )
                a1 = softmax(res.x)
            except Exception:
                a1 = a0
            sc = score_array(a1)
            consider(a1)
            add_top(sc, a1)

    top.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    for _, a, n in top[:16]:
        if time.monotonic() >= polish_deadline:
            break
        try:
            ap, scp = polish(a, n, polish_deadline, max_iter=700)
            consider(ap)
            add_top(scp, ap)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if best_score < 1.28 and time.monotonic() < polish_deadline - 15.0:
            break

    def exact_score(lst):
        try:
            v = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            return v if np.isfinite(v) else float("inf")
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")

    ans = None
    ans_sc = float("inf")
    if best_list is not None:
        lst = [float(x) for x in best_list]
        sc = exact_score(lst)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            ans, ans_sc = lst, sc

    psc = exact_score(parent_list)
    if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < ans_sc:
        ans, ans_sc = parent_list, psc

    if ans is None:
        uols = [1.0]
        if exact_score(uols) == float("inf"):
            uols = [1.0] * 4
        ans = uols
        ans_sc = exact_score(ans)

    if not np.isfinite(ans_sc) or not ans:
        return [1.0]
    return ans
```
